Defining Functions

test_enrichment <- function(comparisons, TopTable,gene_list) {
  
  results <- lapply(comparisons, function(group) {
    
    test_genes <- TopTable %>%
      filter(Comparisons == group) %>%
      distinct(Symbol)
    
    deg_genes <- TopTable %>%
      filter(Comparisons == group,
             adj.P.Val < 0.05) %>%
      distinct(Symbol)
    
    not_deg_genes <- setdiff(test_genes$Symbol, deg_genes$Symbol)
    
    tab <- matrix(
      c(
        sum(deg_genes$Symbol %in% gene_list),
        sum(deg_genes$Symbol %notin% gene_list),
        sum(not_deg_genes %in% gene_list),
        sum(not_deg_genes %notin% gene_list)
      ),
      nrow = 2,
      byrow = TRUE
    )
    
    fisher <- fisher.test(tab)
    
    data.frame(
      Comparison = group,
      KZFP_DEG = tab[1,1],
      NonKZFP_DEG = tab[1,2],
      KZFP_not_DEG = tab[2,1],
      NonKZFP_not_DEG = tab[2,2],
      Odds_Ratio = fisher$estimate,
      Fisher_p = fisher$p.value
    )
  })
  
  do.call(rbind, results)
}

#KZFP_enrichment_fate_function
test_fate_enrichment <- function(comparisons, TopTable_Fate, gene_list) {
  
  results <- lapply(comparisons, function(group) {
    
    test_genes <- TopTable_Fate %>%
      filter(Comparisons == group) %>%
      distinct(Symbol, Fate)
    
    
    fate_tests <- list(
      Chimp_Only = "Chimp_Only",
      Human_Only = "Human_Only",
      Shared = "Shared"
    )
    
    
    do.call(rbind, lapply(names(fate_tests), function(fate_name) {
      
      fate_genes <- test_genes %>%
        filter(Fate == fate_tests[[fate_name]]) %>%
        pull(Symbol)
      
      non_fate_genes <- test_genes %>%
        filter(Fate != fate_tests[[fate_name]]) %>%
        pull(Symbol)
      
      
      tab <- matrix(
        c(
          sum(fate_genes %in% gene_list),
          sum(fate_genes %notin% gene_list),
          sum(non_fate_genes %in% gene_list),
          sum(non_fate_genes %notin% gene_list)
        ),
        nrow = 2,
        byrow = TRUE
      )
      
      fisher <- fisher.test(tab)
      
      data.frame(
        Comparison = group,
        Fate = fate_name,
        KZFP_Fate = tab[1,1],
        NonKZFP_Fate = tab[1,2],
        KZFP_nonFate = tab[2,1],
        NonKZFP_nonFate = tab[2,2],
        Odds_Ratio = fisher$estimate,
        Fisher_p = fisher$p.value
      )
      
    }))
  })
  
  do.call(rbind, results)
}

#KZFP_enrichment_fate_function
test_fate_enrichment_HvC <- function(comparisons, TopTable_Fate, gene_list) {
  
  results <- lapply(comparisons, function(group) {
    
    test_genes <- TopTable_Fate %>%
      filter(Comparisons == group) %>%
      distinct(Symbol, Fate)
    
    
    fate_tests <- list(
      Shared_all = "Shared_all",
      Shared_partial = "Shared_partial",
      Specific = "Specific",
      NonDeg = "Non-Deg"
    )
    
    
    do.call(rbind, lapply(names(fate_tests), function(fate_name) {
      
      fate_genes <- test_genes %>%
        filter(Fate == fate_tests[[fate_name]]) %>%
        pull(Symbol)
      
      non_fate_genes <- test_genes %>%
        filter(Fate != fate_tests[[fate_name]]) %>%
        pull(Symbol)
      
      
      tab <- matrix(
        c(
          sum(fate_genes %in% gene_list),
          sum(fate_genes %notin% gene_list),
          sum(non_fate_genes %in% gene_list),
          sum(non_fate_genes %notin% gene_list)
        ),
        nrow = 2,
        byrow = TRUE
      )
      
      fisher <- fisher.test(tab)
      
      data.frame(
        Comparison = group,
        Fate = fate_name,
        KZFP_Fate = tab[1,1],
        NonKZFP_Fate = tab[1,2],
        KZFP_nonFate = tab[2,1],
        NonKZFP_nonFate = tab[2,2],
        Odds_Ratio = fisher$estimate,
        Fisher_p = fisher$p.value
      )
      
    }))
  })
  
  do.call(rbind, results)
}
# Fisher Test Odds Ratio plotting

plot_OR_cluster <- function(results, cluster_to_plot, ylim = c(0, 2)) {

  plot_data <- results %>%
    filter(Cluster %in% cluster_to_plot) %>%
    mutate(
      label = ifelse(FDR < 0.05, "*", "")
    )

  ggplot(plot_data, aes(Cluster, Odds_Ratio)) +
    geom_point(size = 3) +
    geom_text(
      aes(label = label),
      vjust = -1
    ) +
    geom_hline(yintercept = 1, linetype = "dashed") +
    coord_cartesian(ylim = ylim) +
    theme_bw() +
    theme(
      axis.text.x = element_text(angle = 45, hjust = 1)
    ) +
    labs(
      x = "CorMotif Cluster",
      y = "Odds Ratio"
    )
}

#FischerTest_OddsRation_plotting

plot_OR <- function(results, group_to_plot, ylim = c(-2, 2)) {

  plot_data <- results %>%
    filter(Comparison_Group %in% group_to_plot) %>%
    mutate(
      label = ifelse(FDR < 0.05, "*", ""),
      log2_OR = log2(Odds_Ratio)
    )

  ggplot(plot_data, aes(Comparison, Odds_Ratio)) +
    geom_point(size = 3) +
    geom_text(
      aes(label = label),
      vjust = -1
    ) +
    geom_hline(yintercept = 1, linetype = "dashed") +
    coord_cartesian(ylim = ylim) +
    theme_bw() +
    theme(
      axis.text.x = element_text(angle = 45, hjust = 1)
    ) +
  labs(
      x = NULL,
      y = "Odds Ratio",
      title = paste0("Odds ratio of ", testing," for ", group_to_plot)
    )
}

##Cluster Enrichemnt 
cluster_enrichment_Unassigned <- function(clusters, TopTable, gene_list) {
  
  results <- lapply(clusters, function(cluster) {
    
    cluster_genes <- TopTable %>%
      filter(Cluster == cluster) %>%
      distinct(Symbol) %>%
      pull(Symbol)
    
    unassigned_genes <- TopTable %>%
      filter(Cluster == "Unassigned") %>%
      distinct(Symbol) %>%
      pull(Symbol)
    
    tab <- matrix(
      c(
        sum(cluster_genes %in% gene_list),
        sum(!cluster_genes %in% gene_list),
        sum(unassigned_genes %in% gene_list),
        sum(!unassigned_genes %in% gene_list)
      ),
      nrow = 2,
      byrow = TRUE
    )
    
    fisher <- fisher.test(tab)
    
    data.frame(
      Cluster = cluster,
      KZFP_Cluster = tab[1,1],
      NonKZFP_Cluster = tab[1,2],
      KZFP_Unassigned = tab[2,1],
      NonKZFP_Unassigned = tab[2,2],
      Odds_Ratio = fisher$estimate,
      Fisher_p = fisher$p.value
    )
  })
  
  do.call(rbind, results)
}

##Cluster Enrichemnt 
cluster_enrichment_NoResponse <- function(clusters, TopTable, list) {
  
  results <- lapply(clusters, function(cluster) {
    
    cluster_genes <- TopTable %>%
      filter(Cluster == cluster) %>%
      distinct(Symbol) %>%
      pull(Symbol)
    
    unassigned_genes <- TopTable %>%
      filter(Cluster == "cluster1") %>%
      distinct(Symbol) %>%
      pull(Symbol)
    
    tab <- matrix(
      c(
        sum(cluster_genes %in% list),
        sum(!cluster_genes %in% list),
        sum(unassigned_genes %in% list),
        sum(!unassigned_genes %in% list)
      ),
      nrow = 2,
      byrow = TRUE
    )
    
    fisher <- fisher.test(tab)
    
    data.frame(
      Cluster = cluster,
      List_Cluster = tab[1,1],
      NonList_Cluster = tab[1,2],
      List_Cluster1 = tab[2,1],
      NonList_Cluster1 = tab[2,2],
      Odds_Ratio = fisher$estimate,
      Fisher_p = fisher$p.value
    )
  })
  
  do.call(rbind, results)
}


#FischerTest_fate_OddsRation_plotting

plot_Fate_OR <- function(results, ylim = c(0, 2)) {

  plot_data <- results %>%
    mutate(
      label = ifelse(FDR < 0.05, "*", ""),
      log2_OR = log2(Odds_Ratio)
    )

  ggplot(plot_data, aes(x = Comparison, y = Odds_Ratio)) +
    geom_point(size = 3) +
    geom_text(
      aes(label = label),
      vjust = -1
    ) +
    geom_hline(
      yintercept = 1,
      linetype = "dashed"
    ) +
    facet_wrap(~Fate) +
    coord_cartesian(ylim = ylim) +
    theme_bw() +
    theme(
      axis.text.x = element_text(angle = 45, hjust = 1)
    ) +
    labs(
      x = "HvC developmental comparison",
      y = "Odds Ratio",
      title = "Enrichment by HvC DEG fate"
    )
}

#Volcano_Plot_Function

generate_volcano_plot <- function(toptable, title) {
  
  # #check for entrezid 
  # if(!"Entrez_ID" %in% colnames(toptable)) stop("Entrez_ID col not present")
  # 
  #make significance labels
  
  toptable <- toptable %>% 
    mutate(Significance = case_when(
      logFC > 0 & adj.P.Val < 0.05 ~ "Upregulated",
      logFC < 0 & adj.P.Val < 0.05 ~ "Downregulated",
      TRUE  ~ "Not Significant"
    ))
  
  #factor significance
  toptable$Significance <- factor(
    toptable$Significance, 
    levels = c("Upregulated", 
               "Not Significant", 
               "Downregulated")
  )
  
  #count genes in each category
  upgenes <- sum(toptable$Significance == "Upregulated")
  nsgenes <- sum(toptable$Significance == "Not Significant")
  downgenes <- sum(toptable$Significance == "Downregulated")
  
  
  #labels for legend
  legend_lab <- c(
    paste0("Upregulated: ", upgenes),
    paste0("Not Significant: ", nsgenes),
    paste0("Downregulated: ", downgenes)
  )
  
  #colors 
  color_map <- c("Upregulated" = "blue",
                 "Not Significant" = "grey30",
                 "Downregulated" = "red")
  
  #generate volcano plots
  p <- ggplot(toptable, aes(x = logFC, 
                            y = -log10(P.Value),
                            color = Significance)) +
    geom_point_rast(alpha = 0.8, size = 3) +
    scale_color_manual(values = color_map, 
                       labels = legend_lab,
                       breaks = c("Upregulated",
                                  "Not Significant", 
                                  "Downregulated"))  +
    xlim(-20,20) +
    labs(title = title, 
         x = expression("log"[2]*"FC"),
         y = expression("-log"[10]*"p-value")) +
    # theme_custom() +
    theme(legend.position = "right")
  
  return(p)
}



#FC_Plot_Function_sig_trajectory_No4

plot_cluster_fc_sig_trajectory_No4 <- function(gene_vector, cluster_name, top_table) {

  df <- top_table %>%
    dplyr::filter(Gene %in% gene_vector) %>%
    mutate(Comparisons = factor(Comparisons, levels = c(
      "Day2_vs_Day0","Day5_vs_Day2","Day15_vs_Day5","Day30_vs_Day15"
    )))

  cat(cluster_name, ":", nrow(df), "rows,",
      length(unique(df$Gene)), "unique genes\n")

  # ✅ Skip if no data
  if (nrow(df) == 0) {
    message(cluster_name, ": no data — skipping")
    return(NULL)
  }

  # ✅ Skip if only one comparison present
  if (length(unique(df$Comparisons)) < 2) {
    message(cluster_name, ": only one comparison — skipping stats")
    return(NULL)
  }

  # Stats
  kw <- kruskal.test(logFC ~ Comparisons, data = df)

  pw_kw <- pairwise.wilcox.test(
    df$logFC,
    df$Comparisons,
    p.adjust.method = "BH"
  )

  print(pw_kw)

  p <- ggplot(df, aes(x = Comparisons, y = logFC)) +
    geom_boxplot(aes(fill = Comparisons)) +
    theme_bw() +
    xlab(" ") +
    ylab("logFC") +
    ggtitle(
      paste0(
        cluster_name,
        " Trajectroy DEG FC Comparisons\nKruskal–Wallis p = ",
        signif(kw$p.value, 3)
      )
    ) +
    coord_cartesian(ylim = c(-2, 2)) +
    theme(
      plot.title = element_text(size = rel(1.3), hjust = 0.5),
      axis.title = element_text(size = 15),
      axis.text.x = element_text(size = 8, angle = 15)
    )

  return(p)
}

#get_directional_genes

get_fc_directions <- function(traj_label, coef_name, comparison_label,
                              top_table_all, fit_obj, p_cutoff = 0.05, gene_set = NULL) {

  # ---------------------------
  # trajectory FC data
  # ---------------------------
  df_fc <- top_table_all %>%
    dplyr::filter(
      Comparisons %in% c(
        paste0("H_", comparison_label),
        paste0("C_", comparison_label)
      )
    )

  df_dir <- df_fc %>%
    dplyr::mutate(Species = ifelse(grepl("^H_", Comparisons), "H", "C")) %>%
    dplyr::group_by(Gene, Species) %>%
    dplyr::summarise(logFC = mean(logFC, na.rm = TRUE), .groups = "drop") %>%
    tidyr::pivot_wider(names_from = Species, values_from = logFC) %>%
    dplyr::filter(!is.na(H), !is.na(C)) %>%
    dplyr::distinct(Gene, .keep_all = TRUE)

  # ---------------------------
  # KEY FILTER: DEGs in BOTH species
  # ---------------------------
  if (!is.null(gene_set)) {
    df_dir <- df_dir %>%
      dplyr::filter(Gene %in% gene_set)
  }

  # ---------------------------
  # direction classification
  # ---------------------------
  df_combo <- df_dir %>%
    dplyr::mutate(
      direction = dplyr::case_when(
        H > 0 & C < 0 ~ "H_pos_C_neg",
        H < 0 & C > 0 ~ "H_neg_C_pos",
        H > 0 & C > 0 ~ "Both_pos",
        H < 0 & C < 0 ~ "Both_neg",
        TRUE ~ "Other"
      ),
      direction_group = dplyr::case_when(
        H * C > 0 ~ "Same_direction",
        H * C < 0 ~ "Opposite_direction",
        TRUE ~ "Other"
      )
    )

  # ---------------------------
  # output
  # ---------------------------
  list(
    all = df_combo,
    H_pos_C_neg = dplyr::filter(df_combo, direction == "H_pos_C_neg"),
    H_neg_C_pos = dplyr::filter(df_combo, direction == "H_neg_C_pos"),
    Both_pos = dplyr::filter(df_combo, direction == "Both_pos"),
    Both_neg = dplyr::filter(df_combo, direction == "Both_neg"),

    Same_direction = dplyr::filter(df_combo, direction_group == "Same_direction"),
    Opposite_direction = dplyr::filter(df_combo, direction_group == "Opposite_direction"),

    Other = dplyr::filter(df_combo, direction == "Other"),

    n_sig = length(gene_set)
  )
}

#get_directional_genes_HvC1

get_fc_directions <- function(traj_label, coef_name, comparison_label,
                                 top_table_all, fit_obj, p_cutoff = 0.05) {

  # ---------------------------
  # DE genes
  # ---------------------------
  tt <- topTable(fit_obj, coef = coef_name, number = Inf)

  sig_tt <- tt %>%
    dplyr::filter(adj.P.Val < p_cutoff) %>%
    tibble::rownames_to_column("Gene") %>%
    dplyr::distinct(Gene, .keep_all = TRUE)

  # ---------------------------
  # HvC FC data
  # ---------------------------
  df_fc <- top_table_all %>%
    dplyr::filter(Comparisons == comparison_label) %>%
    dplyr::distinct(Gene, .keep_all = TRUE)

  # ---------------------------
  # classify direction
  # ---------------------------
  df_combo <- df_fc %>%
  dplyr::inner_join(sig_tt, by = "Gene") %>%
  dplyr::mutate(
    direction = dplyr::case_when(
      logFC.x > 0 ~ "Increased",
      logFC.x < 0 ~ "Decreased",
      TRUE ~ "No_difference"
    ),
    magnitude = abs(logFC.x)
  )

  # ---------------------------
  # output
  # ---------------------------
  list(
    all = df_combo,
    Increased = dplyr::filter(df_combo, direction == "Increased"),
    Decreased = dplyr::filter(df_combo, direction == "Decreased"),
    n_sig = nrow(sig_tt)
  )
}


#####Log2cpm Boxplots
plot_log2cpm_boxplot <- function(
    log2cpm,
    metadata,
    group,
    testing,
    n,
    levels = NULL,
    ylim = c(-5, 10)
) {
  
  # Create long-format data
  df_plot <- as.data.frame(log2cpm) %>%
    mutate(Gene = rownames(log2cpm)) %>%
    pivot_longer(
      cols = -Gene,
      names_to = "Sample",
      values_to = "log2cpm"
    ) %>%
    left_join(
      metadata %>% 
        dplyr::select(SampleName, all_of(group)),
      by = c("Sample" = "SampleName")
    )
  
  # Set factor levels if provided
  if (!is.null(levels)) {
    df_plot[[group]] <- factor(
      df_plot[[group]],
      levels = levels
    )
  }
  
  # Plot
  boxplot(
    df_plot$log2cpm ~ df_plot[[group]],
    main = paste0(
      "Boxplot of ", testing, " log2cpm\n",
      "(n = ", n, ")"
    ),
    xlab = group,
    ylab = "log2 CPM",
    ylim = ylim,
    las = 2
  )
}

# --------------------------------------------------
# InteractionAtLeastOne
# --------------------------------------------------

Inter_02_genes <- unique(RNA_sets$Sig_Sp_inter_Traj02$DEGs$Gene)
Inter_25_genes <- unique(RNA_sets$Sig_Sp_inter_Traj25$DEGs$Gene)
Inter_515_genes <- unique(RNA_sets$Sig_Sp_inter_Traj515$DEGs$Gene)
Inter_1530_genes <- unique(RNA_sets$Sig_Sp_inter_Traj1530$DEGs$Gene)

InteractionAtLeastOne <- unique(c(
  Inter_02_genes,
  Inter_25_genes,
  Inter_515_genes,
  Inter_1530_genes
))

# Universe = all genes represented in TopTable
test_genes <- unique(TopTable$Symbol)

# Convert InteractionAtLeastOne Ensembl IDs to gene symbols
InteractionAtLeastOne_symbols <- TopTable %>%
  filter(Gene %in% InteractionAtLeastOne) %>%
  pull(Symbol) %>%
  unique()

# InteractionAtLeastOne genes are DEGs by definition
deg_genes <- intersect(
  InteractionAtLeastOne_symbols,
  test_genes
)

# Everything else in the universe is non-DEG
not_deg_genes <- setdiff(
  test_genes,
  deg_genes
)

tab <- matrix(
  c(
    sum(deg_genes %in% gene_list),
    sum(deg_genes %notin% gene_list),
    sum(not_deg_genes %in% gene_list),
    sum(not_deg_genes %notin% gene_list)
  ),
  nrow = 2,
  byrow = TRUE
)

fisher <- fisher.test(tab)

InteractionAtLeastOne_result <- data.frame(
  Comparison = "InteractionAtLeastOne",
  KZFP_DEG = tab[1,1],
  NonKZFP_DEG = tab[1,2],
  KZFP_not_DEG = tab[2,1],
  NonKZFP_not_DEG = tab[2,2],
  Odds_Ratio = fisher$estimate,
  Fisher_p = fisher$p.value
)

# Add to existing results
Enrichment_results <- bind_rows(
  Enrichment_results,
  InteractionAtLeastOne_result
)

# BH correction across the complete set of comparisons
Enrichment_results$FDR <- p.adjust(
  Enrichment_results$Fisher_p,
  method = "BH"
)

  "HvC_Day0",

  "Sig_Sp_inter_Traj02",
  "Sig_Sp_inter_Traj25",
  "Sig_Sp_inter_Traj515",
  "Sig_Sp_inter_Traj1530",
  "InteractionAtLeastOne"
)


Enrichment_results <- Enrichment_results %>%
  mutate(
    Comparison = factor(
      Comparison,
      levels = comparison_order
    ),
    Comparison_Group = case_when(
      Comparison == "InteractionAtLeastOne" ~ "Interaction",
      grepl("^HvC", Comparison) ~ "Species",
      grepl("inter", Comparison) ~ "Interaction",
      grepl("^[HC]_Day", Comparison) ~ "Trajectory",
      TRUE ~ "Other"
    ),
    Significant = FDR < 0.05
  )

# --------------------------------------------------
# KZFP enrichment with confidence intervals
# --------------------------------------------------

library(dplyr)
library(ggplot2)

KZFPs_list <- readRDS("data/TEs/KZFPs_list.RDS")

TopTable <- readRDS(
  "data/DGE/TopTable_Symbols_Filt_logcpm_G1_SpTp_RNA_fc_NoRep_No4.RDS"
)

RNA_sets <- readRDS(
  "data/DGE/RNA_sets_Filt_logcpm_G1_SpTp_RNA_fc_NoRep_No4.RDS"
)

gene_list <- KZFPs_list


# --------------------------------------------------
# Enrichment function
# --------------------------------------------------

test_KZFP_enrichment_CI <- function(comparisons, TopTable, gene_list) {

  test_genes <- unique(na.omit(TopTable$Symbol))

  results <- lapply(comparisons, function(comparison) {

    deg_genes <- TopTable %>%
      filter(
        Comparisons == comparison,
        adj.P.Val < 0.05
      ) %>%
      pull(Symbol) %>%
      unique() %>%
      na.omit()

    not_deg_genes <- setdiff(test_genes, deg_genes)

    tab <- matrix(
      c(
        sum(deg_genes %in% gene_list),
        sum(deg_genes %notin% gene_list),
        sum(not_deg_genes %in% gene_list),
        sum(not_deg_genes %notin% gene_list)
      ),
      nrow = 2,
      byrow = TRUE
    )

    fisher <- fisher.test(tab)

    data.frame(
      Comparison = comparison,
      KZFP_DEG = tab[1, 1],
      NonKZFP_DEG = tab[1, 2],
      KZFP_not_DEG = tab[2, 1],
      NonKZFP_not_DEG = tab[2, 2],
      Odds_Ratio = as.numeric(fisher$estimate),
      CI_lower = fisher$conf.int[1],
      CI_upper = fisher$conf.int[2],
      Fisher_p = fisher$p.value
    )
  })

  bind_rows(results)
}


# --------------------------------------------------
# Run enrichment
# --------------------------------------------------

comparisons <- unique(TopTable$Comparisons)

Enrichment_results_CI <- test_KZFP_enrichment_CI(
  comparisons = comparisons,
  TopTable = TopTable,
  gene_list = gene_list
)

  mutate(
    Comparison = factor(
      Comparison,
      levels = comparison_order
    ),
    Comparison_Group = case_when(
      Comparison == "InteractionAtLeastOne" ~ "Interaction",
      grepl("^HvC", Comparison) ~ "Species",
      grepl("inter", Comparison) ~ "Interaction",
      grepl("^[HC]_Day", Comparison) ~ "Trajectory",
      TRUE ~ "Other"
    ),
    Significant = FDR < 0.05
  )

# --------------------------------------------------
# Plot odds ratios with 95% CI
# New function: plot_KZFP_OR_CI
# --------------------------------------------------

plot_KZFP_OR_CI <- function(df, comparison_group, ylim = NULL) {

  plot_df <- df %>%
    filter(Comparison_Group == comparison_group) %>%
    mutate(
      Significant = factor(
        ifelse(FDR < 0.05, "Significant", "Not significant"),
        levels = c("Not significant", "Significant")
      )
    )

  p <- ggplot(
    plot_df,
    aes(
      x = Comparison,
      y = Odds_Ratio
    )
  ) +
    geom_hline(
      yintercept = 1,
      linetype = "dashed",
      color = "grey50"
    ) +
    geom_errorbar(
      aes(
        ymin = CI_lower,
        ymax = CI_upper
      ),
      width = 0.2
    ) +
    geom_point(
      aes(
        shape = Significant,
        fill = Significant
      ),
      size = 3,
      color = "black"
    ) +
    scale_shape_manual(
      values = c(
        "Not significant" = 21,
        "Significant" = 21
      )
    ) +
    scale_fill_manual(
      values = c(
        "Not significant" = "white",
        "Significant" = "black"
      )
    ) +
    theme_bw() +
    labs(
      x = NULL,
      y = "Odds Ratio",
      title = comparison_group,
      shape = "FDR < 0.05",
      fill = "FDR < 0.05"
    ) +
    theme(
      axis.text.x = element_text(
        angle = 45,
        hjust = 1
      )
    )

  if (!is.null(ylim)) {
    p <- p + coord_cartesian(ylim = ylim)
  }

  return(p)
}


# --------------------------------------------------
# Generate plots
# --------------------------------------------------

plot_KZFP_OR_CI(
  Enrichment_results_CI,
  "Species",
  ylim = c(0, 2)
)

plot_KZFP_OR_CI(
  Enrichment_results_CI,
  "Trajectory",
  ylim = c(0, 3)
)

plot_KZFP_OR_CI(
  Enrichment_results_CI,
  "Interaction",
  ylim = c(0, 2.5)
)

# # --------------------------------------------------
# # Plot odds ratios with 95% CI
# # New function: plot_KZFP_OR_CI
# # --------------------------------------------------
# 
plot_ZNF_OR_CI <- function(df, comparison_group, ylim = NULL) {

  plot_df <- df %>%
    filter(Comparison_Group == comparison_group) %>%
    mutate(
      Significant = factor(
        ifelse(FDR < 0.05, "Significant", "Not significant"),
        levels = c("Not significant", "Significant")
      )
    )

  p <- ggplot(
    plot_df,
    aes(
      x = Comparison,
      y = Odds_Ratio
    )
  ) +
    geom_hline(
      yintercept = 1,
      linetype = "dashed",
      color = "grey50"
    ) +
    geom_errorbar(
      aes(
        ymin = CI_lower,
        ymax = CI_upper
      ),
      width = 0.2
    ) +
    geom_point(
      aes(
        shape = Significant,
        fill = Significant
      ),
      size = 3,
      color = "black"
    ) +
    scale_shape_manual(
      values = c(
        "Not significant" = 21,
        "Significant" = 21
      )
    ) +
    scale_fill_manual(
      values = c(
        "Not significant" = "white",
        "Significant" = "black"
      )
    ) +
    theme_bw() +
    labs(
      x = NULL,
      y = "Odds Ratio",
      title = comparison_group,
      shape = "FDR < 0.05",
      fill = "FDR < 0.05"
    ) +
    theme(
      axis.text.x = element_text(
        angle = 45,
        hjust = 1
      )
    )

  if (!is.null(ylim)) {
    p <- p + coord_cartesian(ylim = ylim)
  }

  return(p)
}

# # --------------------------------------------------
# # Plot odds ratios with 95% CI
# # New function: plot_KZFP_OR_CI
# # --------------------------------------------------
# 
plot_TF_OR_CI <- function(df, comparison_group, ylim = NULL) {

  plot_df <- df %>%
    filter(Comparison_Group == comparison_group) %>%
    mutate(
      Significant = factor(
        ifelse(FDR < 0.05, "Significant", "Not significant"),
        levels = c("Not significant", "Significant")
      )
    )

  p <- ggplot(
    plot_df,
    aes(
      x = Comparison,
      y = Odds_Ratio
    )
  ) +
    geom_hline(
      yintercept = 1,
      linetype = "dashed",
      color = "grey50"
    ) +
    geom_errorbar(
      aes(
        ymin = CI_lower,
        ymax = CI_upper
      ),
      width = 0.2
    ) +
    geom_point(
      aes(
        shape = Significant,
        fill = Significant
      ),
      size = 3,
      color = "black"
    ) +
    scale_shape_manual(
      values = c(
        "Not significant" = 21,
        "Significant" = 21
      )
    ) +
    scale_fill_manual(
      values = c(
        "Not significant" = "white",
        "Significant" = "black"
      )
    ) +
    theme_bw() +
    labs(
      x = NULL,
      y = "Odds Ratio",
      title = comparison_group,
      shape = "FDR < 0.05",
      fill = "FDR < 0.05"
    ) +
    theme(
      axis.text.x = element_text(
        angle = 45,
        hjust = 1
      )
    )

  if (!is.null(ylim)) {
    p <- p + coord_cartesian(ylim = ylim)
  }

  return(p)
}

metadata <- readRDS("data/Raw_Data/concat/RNA_Metadata_No4_NoRep.RDS")
RNA_Metadata <- readRDS("data/Raw_Data/concat/RNA_Metadata_No4_NoRep.RDS")
fc <- readRDS("data/QC/concat/Filt_logcpm_G1_SpTp_RNA_fc_No4.RDS")
KZFPs_list <- readRDS("data/TEs/KZFPs_list.RDS")
ZNFs <- readRDS("data/TEs/ZNFs.RDS")
tf_df <- readRDS("data/TEs/tf_df.RDS")
fc_df <- readRDS("data/Raw_Data/concat/RNA_fc_df.RDS")

metadata <- RNA_Metadata %>% 
  filter(Timepoint != "Day4")

# Get Ensembl IDs corresponding to your TF symbols
TFs_list <- tf_df$Gene

TFs <- fc_df %>%
  filter(Symbol %in% TFs_list) %>%
  pull(Ensemble) %>%
  unique()

# Get Ensembl IDs corresponding to your KZFP symbols
ZNFs_list <- ZNFs$`Approved symbol`

ZNfs <- fc_df %>%
  filter(Symbol %in% ZNFs_list) %>%
  pull(Ensemble) %>%
  unique()

# Get Ensembl IDs corresponding to your KZFP symbols
KZFPs <- fc_df %>%
  filter(Symbol %in% KZFPs_list) %>%
  pull(Ensemble) %>%
  unique()

Expressed_genes <- rownames(fc)

gene_sets <- list(
  Expr_genes = Expressed_genes,
  Expr_TFs = TFs,
  Expr_ZNfs = ZNfs,
  Expr_KZFPs = KZFPs
)


results <- list()

for (i in seq_along(gene_sets)) {

  gene_set <- gene_sets[[i]]
  cluster_name <- names(gene_sets)[[i]]

  print(cluster_name)
  print(length(gene_set))

  # Keep genes in cluster
  counts <- fc[rownames(fc) %in% gene_set, ]

  # Metadata
  metadata_i <- RNA_Metadata

  metadata_i <- metadata_i[
    match(colnames(counts), metadata_i$SampleName),
  ]

  metadata_i <- metadata_i %>%
    filter(Individual != "H4R") %>%
    filter(Timepoint != "Day4")

  counts <- counts %>%
    dplyr::select(-contains("R")) %>%
    dplyr::select(-contains("Day4"))

  # Group labels
  metadata_i$dgelist <- c(
    rep(c(
      "Human_Day0",
      "Human_Day2",
      "Human_Day5",
      "Human_Day15",
      "Human_Day30",
      "Chimp_Day0",
      "Chimp_Day2",
      "Chimp_Day5",
      "Chimp_Day15",
      "Chimp_Day30"
    ), 7)
  )

  # DGEList
  dge <- DGEList(counts = counts)

  dge$samples$group <- factor(metadata_i$dgelist)

  dge <- calcNormFactors(
    dge,
    method = "TMM"
  )

  # log2 CPM
  log2cpm <- cpm(
    dge,
    log = TRUE
  )

  # Match metadata
  metadata_i <- metadata_i[
    match(
      colnames(log2cpm),
      metadata_i$SampleName
    ),
  ]

  stopifnot(
    all(
      metadata_i$SampleName ==
        colnames(log2cpm)
    )
  )

  stopifnot(
    !any(
      is.na(metadata_i$SampleName)
    )
  )

  # Correlation
  Cor_log2cpm <- cor(
    log2cpm,
    method = "spearman"
  )

  # Sample information
  sample_info <- data.frame(
    sample = colnames(Cor_log2cpm),
    species = metadata_i$Species,
    timepoint = metadata_i$Timepoint
  )

  # Long format
  cor_long <- as.data.frame(Cor_log2cpm) %>%
    mutate(
      sample1 = rownames(Cor_log2cpm)
    ) %>%
    pivot_longer(
      -sample1,
      names_to = "sample2",
      values_to = "cor"
    ) %>%
    filter(sample1 != sample2) %>%
    left_join(
      sample_info,
      by = c("sample1" = "sample")
    ) %>%
    dplyr::rename(
      species1 = species,
      timepoint1 = timepoint
    ) %>%
    left_join(
      sample_info,
      by = c("sample2" = "sample")
    ) %>%
    dplyr::rename(
      species2 = species,
      timepoint2 = timepoint
    )

  # Same-day cross-species
  same_day_cross <- cor_long %>%
    filter(
      species1 != species2,
      timepoint1 == timepoint2
    ) %>%
    filter(species1 == "H") %>%
    mutate(
      timepoint1 = factor(
        timepoint1,
        levels = c(
          "Day0",
          "Day2",
          "Day5",
          "Day15",
          "Day30"
        )
      )
    )

  # Summary
  summary_by_day <- same_day_cross %>%
    group_by(timepoint1) %>%
    summarise(
      mean_cor = mean(cor, na.rm = TRUE),
      sd_cor = sd(cor, na.rm = TRUE),
      median_cor = median(cor, na.rm = TRUE),
      n = n(),
      .groups = "drop"
    )

  # Kruskal-Wallis
  kruskal_res <- kruskal.test(
    cor ~ timepoint1,
    data = same_day_cross
  )

  # Pairwise Wilcoxon
  stat.test <- same_day_cross %>%
    pairwise_wilcox_test(
      cor ~ timepoint1,
      p.adjust.method = "BH"
    ) %>%
    add_significance("p.adj") %>%
    add_y_position() %>%
    filter(p.adj <= 0.05)

  # Save results
  results[[cluster_name]] <- list(
    genes = gene_set,
    n_genes = length(gene_set),
    same_day_cross = same_day_cross,
    summary = summary_by_day,
    kruskal = kruskal_res,
    pairwise = stat.test
  )
}
[1] "Expr_genes"
[1] 15815
[1] "Expr_TFs"
[1] 1378
[1] "Expr_ZNfs"
[1] 717
[1] "Expr_KZFPs"
[1] 336
# Combine cluster summaries
all_cluster_summary <- bind_rows(
  lapply(names(results), function(cluster_name) {

    results[[cluster_name]]$summary %>%
      mutate(cluster = cluster_name)

  })
)

# Plot each cluster individually
for (cluster_name in names(results)) {

  plot_data <- results[[cluster_name]]$same_day_cross
  stat.test <- results[[cluster_name]]$pairwise

  plot <- ggplot(
    plot_data,
    aes(
      x = timepoint1,
      y = cor
    )
  ) +

    geom_jitter(
      width = 0.08,
      alpha = 0.3
    ) +

    stat_summary(
      aes(group = 1),
      fun = median,
      geom = "line",
      linewidth = 1
    ) +

    stat_summary(
      fun = median,
      geom = "point",
      size = 3
    ) +

    stat_pvalue_manual(
      stat.test,
      label = "p.adj.signif",
      step.increase = 0.04
    ) +

    coord_cartesian(
      ylim = c(0.7, 1.00)
    ) +

    labs(
      title = paste(
        "Cross-species correlation -",
        cluster_name
      ),
      x = "Timepoint",
      y = "Median Spearman correlation"
    ) +

    theme_classic()

  print(plot)
}

# Plot all clusters together
plot_all <- ggplot(
  all_cluster_summary,
  aes(
    x = timepoint1,
    y = median_cor,
    group = cluster,
    color = cluster
  )
) +

  geom_line(
    linewidth = 1
  ) +

  geom_point(
    size = 2
  ) +

  coord_cartesian(
    ylim = c(0.0, 1.00)
  ) +

  labs(
    title = "Cross-species correlation across CorMotif clusters",
    x = "Timepoint",
    y = "Median Spearman correlation",
    color = "Cluster"
  ) +

  theme_classic()

ENSG000001981692    HvC_Day5  ZNF251 -6.9647092 2.966521e-16
 testing_DEG_direction <-  testing_DEG %>%
  mutate(
    Direction = case_when(
      
      # Species contrasts
      grepl("^HvC", Comparisons) & logFC > 0 ~ "Higher in Human",
      grepl("^HvC", Comparisons) & logFC < 0 ~ "Higher in Chimp",
      
      # Human trajectory contrasts
      grepl("^H_Day", Comparisons) & logFC > 0 ~ "Human increased",
      grepl("^H_Day", Comparisons) & logFC < 0 ~ "Human decreased",
      
      # Chimp trajectory contrasts
      grepl("^C_Day", Comparisons) & logFC > 0 ~ "Chimp increased",
      grepl("^C_Day", Comparisons) & logFC < 0 ~ "Chimp decreased",
      
      # Species x trajectory interactions
      grepl("^Sig_Sp_inter", Comparisons) & logFC > 0 ~ "Human-Chimp difference increased",
      grepl("^Sig_Sp_inter", Comparisons) & logFC < 0 ~ "Human-Chimp difference decreased",
      
      TRUE ~ "Unknown"
    )
  ) %>%
  dplyr::count(Comparisons, Direction)
# Load your processed TMM-normalized log2CPM expression data
log2cpm <- readRDS("data/Cormotif/TACC/ToCormotif/Filt_logcpm_G1_SpTp_RNA_log2cpm_No4_TMM.RDS")

library(tidyverse)

# 1. Reshape, parse sample names, and average replicates
plot_data <- log2cpm %>% 
  as.data.frame() %>% 
  rownames_to_column(var = "gene") %>% 
  filter(gene == "ENSG00000196611") %>%  # Or your target gene
  pivot_longer(
    cols = -gene, 
    names_to = "sample", 
    values_to = "log2_cpm"
  ) %>% 
  # Regex to pull out Species (H/C), Individual ID, and Day Number
  # Matches e.g., H20682_Day15 -> Species="H", Individual="20682", Day="15"
  extract(
    sample, 
    into = c("Species", "Individual", "Day"), 
    regex = "^([HC])(\\d+)_Day(\\d+)$"
  ) %>% 
  mutate(
    Day = as.numeric(Day),
    Species = recode(Species, "H" = "Human", "C" = "Chimpanzee")
  ) %>% 
  # Group by Species and Day to calculate the mean across individuals
  group_by(Species, Day) %>% 
  summarise(
    mean_log2_cpm = mean(log2_cpm),
    median_log2_cpm = median(log2_cpm),
    se_log2_cpm = sd(log2_cpm) / sqrt(n()), # Optional: Standard Error for error bars
    .groups = "drop"
  )

# 2. Plot the clean averaged timeline
ggplot(plot_data, aes(x = Day, y = mean_log2_cpm, color = Species, group = Species)) +
  geom_line(linewidth = 1.2) +
  geom_point(size = 3) +
  # Optional: Adds shaded standard error bars to show variance between individuals
  geom_errorbar(aes(ymin = mean_log2_cpm - se_log2_cpm, ymax = mean_log2_cpm + se_log2_cpm), 
                width = 1, alpha = 0.5) +
  scale_color_manual(values = c("Human" = "black", "Chimpanzee" = "grey")) +
  scale_x_continuous(breaks = unique(plot_data$Day)) +
  labs(
    title = "Expression Time Course of ENSG00000196611 (MMP1)",
    subtitle = "Mean Expression Across Biological Replicates",
    x = "Timepoint (Day)",
    y = "Mean Expression Level (log2 CPM)",
    color = "Species"
  ) +
  theme_minimal(base_size = 14) +
  theme(
    plot.title = element_text(face = "bold", hjust = 0.5),
    plot.subtitle = element_text(hjust = 0.5),
    legend.position = "bottom"
  )

# 2. Plot the clean averaged timeline

library(dplyr)
library(ggplot2)

# Split the existing KZFP data into the three comparison types
TopTable_HvC <- TopTable %>%
  filter(grepl("^HvC_", Comparisons))

TopTable_Traj <- TopTable %>%
  filter(grepl("^[HC]_Day", Comparisons))

TopTable_Inter <- TopTable %>%
  filter(grepl("^Sig_Sp_inter_", Comparisons))

TopTable_HvC <- TopTable %>%
  filter(grepl("^HvC_", Comparisons)) %>%
  group_by(Symbol) %>%
  mutate(
    DEG_any = any(!is.na(adj.P.Val) & adj.P.Val < 0.05)
  ) %>%
  ungroup()

ggplot(
  TopTable_HvC,
  aes(
    x = Comparisons,
    y = logFC,
    group = Symbol,
    color = DEG_any
  )
) +
  geom_line(alpha = 0.5) +
  geom_point(size = 1.5) +
  theme_bw() +
  scale_color_manual(
    values = c(
      "FALSE" = "grey70",
      "TRUE" = "darkblue"
    ),
    labels = c(
      "FALSE" = "Not DEG",
      "TRUE" = "DEG in ≥1 timepoint"
    )
  ) +
  labs(
    x = "Comparison",
    y = "logFC",
    color = paste0( testing," status"),
    title = paste0("Human vs. Chimpanzee ",  testing," expression")
  ) +
  coord_cartesian(ylim = c(-10, 10)) +
  theme(
    axis.text.x = element_text(angle = 45, hjust = 1)
  )

 testing_DEG_direction <-  testing_DEG %>%
  mutate(
    Direction = case_when(
      
      # Species contrasts
      grepl("^HvC", Comparisons) & logFC > 0 ~ "Higher in Human",
      grepl("^HvC", Comparisons) & logFC < 0 ~ "Higher in Chimp",
      
      # Human trajectory contrasts
      grepl("^H_Day", Comparisons) & logFC > 0 ~ "Human increased",
      grepl("^H_Day", Comparisons) & logFC < 0 ~ "Human decreased",
      
      # Chimp trajectory contrasts
      grepl("^C_Day", Comparisons) & logFC > 0 ~ "Chimp increased",
      grepl("^C_Day", Comparisons) & logFC < 0 ~ "Chimp decreased",
      
      # Species x trajectory interactions
      grepl("^Sig_Sp_inter", Comparisons) & logFC > 0 ~ "Human-Chimp difference increased",
      grepl("^Sig_Sp_inter", Comparisons) & logFC < 0 ~ "Human-Chimp difference decreased",
      
      TRUE ~ "Unknown"
    )
  ) %>%
  dplyr::count(Comparisons, Direction)
TopTable <-readRDS("data/TEs/ZNFs_Toptable.RDS")

 testing <- "ZNF"

library(dplyr)
library(ggplot2)

# Split the existing NZF data into the three comparison types
TopTable_HvC <- TopTable %>%
  filter(grepl("^HvC_", Comparisons))

TopTable_Traj <- TopTable %>%
  filter(grepl("^[HC]_Day", Comparisons))

TopTable_Inter <- TopTable %>%
  filter(grepl("^Sig_Sp_inter_", Comparisons))

TopTable_HvC <- TopTable %>%
  filter(grepl("^HvC_", Comparisons)) %>%
  group_by(Symbol) %>%
  mutate(
    DEG_any = any(!is.na(adj.P.Val) & adj.P.Val < 0.05)
  ) %>%
  ungroup()

ggplot(
  TopTable_HvC,
  aes(
    x = Comparisons,
    y = logFC,
    group = Symbol,
    color = DEG_any
  )
) +
  geom_line(alpha = 0.5) +
  geom_point(size = 1.5) +
  theme_bw() +
  scale_color_manual(
    values = c(
      "FALSE" = "grey70",
      "TRUE" = "darkblue"
    ),
    labels = c(
      "FALSE" = "Not DEG",
      "TRUE" = "DEG in ≥1 timepoint"
    )
  ) +
  labs(
    x = "Comparison",
    y = "logFC",
    color = paste0( testing," status"),
    title = paste0("Human vs. Chimpanzee ",  testing," expression")
  ) +
  coord_cartesian(ylim = c(-10, 10)) +
  theme(
    axis.text.x = element_text(angle = 45, hjust = 1)
  )

TopTable_Human_Traj <- TopTable  %>%
  filter(grepl("^H_Day", Comparisons)) %>%
  group_by(Symbol) %>%
  mutate(
    DEG_any = any(!is.na(adj.P.Val) & adj.P.Val < 0.05)
  ) %>%
  ungroup()

TopTable_Human_Traj$Comparisons <- factor(
  TopTable_Human_Traj$Comparisons,
  levels = c(
    "H_Day02",
    "H_Day25",
    "H_Day515",
    "H_Day1530"
  )
)

ggplot(
  TopTable_Human_Traj,
  aes(
    x = Comparisons,
    y = logFC,
    group = Symbol,
    color = DEG_any
  )
) +
  geom_line(alpha = 0.5) +
  geom_point(size = 1.5) +
  scale_color_manual(
    values = c(
      "FALSE" = "grey70",
      "TRUE" = "darkred"
    ),
    labels = c(
      "FALSE" = "Not DEG",
      "TRUE" = "DEG in ≥1 transition"
    )
  ) +
  geom_hline(yintercept = 0, linetype = "dashed") +
  theme_bw() +
  labs(
    x = "Comparison",
    y = "logFC",
    color = paste0( testing, " status"),
    title = paste0("Human ",  testing," trajectories")
  ) +
  coord_cartesian(ylim = c(-10, 10)) +
  theme(
    axis.text.x = element_text(angle = 45, hjust = 1)
  )

testing <- "TFs"

comparisons <- unique(TopTable$Comparisons)

comparison_order <- c(
  "HvC_Day0",
  "HvC_Day2",
  "HvC_Day5",
  "HvC_Day15",
  "HvC_Day30",
  "H_Day02",
  "H_Day25",
  "H_Day515",
  "H_Day1530",
  "C_Day02",
  "C_Day25",
  "C_Day515",
  "C_Day1530",
  "Sig_Sp_inter_Traj02",
  "Sig_Sp_inter_Traj25",
  "Sig_Sp_inter_Traj515",
  "Sig_Sp_inter_Traj1530"
)

 testing_all <- TopTable %>%
  filter(
    Comparisons %in% comparison_order,
    Symbol %in%  testing_list
  ) %>%
  mutate(
    Comparisons = factor(
      Comparisons,
      levels = comparison_order
    )
  )

ggplot( testing_all, aes(Comparisons, logFC)) +
  geom_boxplot() +
  ggtitle(paste0("Logfc of all ",  testing," expressed")) +
  geom_hline(yintercept = 0, linetype = "dashed") +
  theme_bw() +
  coord_cartesian(ylim = c(-2, 2)) +
  theme(
    axis.text.x = element_text(angle = 45, hjust = 1)
  )

 testing_all$absfc <- abs( testing_all$logFC)

ggplot( testing_all, aes(Comparisons, absfc)) +
  geom_boxplot() +
  ggtitle(paste0("Absolute logfc of all ",  testing," expressed")) +
  geom_hline(yintercept = 0, linetype = "dashed") +
  theme_bw() +
  coord_cartesian(ylim = c(0, 3)) +
  theme(
    axis.text.x = element_text(angle = 45, hjust = 1)

 testing_DEG_direction <-  testing_DEG %>%
  mutate(
    Direction = case_when(
      
      # Species contrasts
      grepl("^HvC", Comparisons) & logFC > 0 ~ "Higher in Human",
      grepl("^HvC", Comparisons) & logFC < 0 ~ "Higher in Chimp",
      
      # Human trajectory contrasts
      grepl("^H_Day", Comparisons) & logFC > 0 ~ "Human increased",
      grepl("^H_Day", Comparisons) & logFC < 0 ~ "Human decreased",
      
      # Chimp trajectory contrasts
      grepl("^C_Day", Comparisons) & logFC > 0 ~ "Chimp increased",
      grepl("^C_Day", Comparisons) & logFC < 0 ~ "Chimp decreased",
      
      # Species x trajectory interactions
      grepl("^Sig_Sp_inter", Comparisons) & logFC > 0 ~ "Human-Chimp difference increased",
      grepl("^Sig_Sp_inter", Comparisons) & logFC < 0 ~ "Human-Chimp difference decreased",
      
      TRUE ~ "Unknown"
    )
  ) %>%
  dplyr::count(Comparisons, Direction)
TopTable <- readRDS("data/DGE/TopTable_Symbols_Filt_logcpm_G1_SpTp_RNA_fc_NoRep_No4.RDS")

TF_df <- readRDS("data/TEs/TF_df.RDS")

TF_list <- TF_df$Gene

TF_all <- TopTable %>%
  filter(
    Comparisons %in% comparison_order,
    Symbol %in% TF_list
  ) %>%
  mutate(
    Comparisons = factor(
      Comparisons,
      levels = comparison_order
    )
  )

library(dplyr)
library(ggplot2)

# Split the existing TF data into the three comparison types
TF_HvC <- TF_all %>%
  filter(grepl("^HvC_", Comparisons))

TF_Traj <- TF_all %>%
  filter(grepl("^[HC]_Day", Comparisons))

TF_Inter <- TF_all %>%
  filter(grepl("^Sig_Sp_inter_", Comparisons))

TF_HvC <- TF_all %>%
  filter(grepl("^HvC_", Comparisons)) %>%
  group_by(Symbol) %>%
  mutate(
    DEG_any = any(!is.na(adj.P.Val) & adj.P.Val < 0.05)
  ) %>%
  ungroup()

ggplot(
  TF_HvC,
  aes(
    x = Comparisons,
    y = logFC,
    group = Symbol,
    color = DEG_any
  )
) +
  geom_line(alpha = 0.5) +
  geom_point(size = 1.5) +
  theme_bw() +
  scale_color_manual(
    values = c(
      "FALSE" = "grey70",
      "TRUE" = "darkblue"
    ),
    labels = c(
      "FALSE" = "Not DEG",
      "TRUE" = "DEG in ≥1 timepoint"
    )
  ) +
  labs(
    x = "Comparison",
    y = "logFC",
    color = "TF status",
    title = "Human vs. Chimpanzee expression of TF"
  ) +
  coord_cartesian(ylim = c(-10, 10)) +
  theme(
    axis.text.x = element_text(angle = 45, hjust = 1)
  )

Alluvial plot

KZFP

TopTable <- readRDS("data/DGE/TopTable_Symbols_Filt_logcpm_G1_SpTp_RNA_fc_NoRep_No4.RDS")

KZFPs <- readRDS("data/TEs/KZFPs.RDS")

gene_list <- KZFPs$Label

testing <- "KZFPs"

comparisons <- unique(TopTable$Comparisons)

comparison_order <- c(
  "HvC_Day0",
  "HvC_Day2",
  "HvC_Day5",
  "HvC_Day15",
  "HvC_Day30",
  "H_Day02",
  "H_Day25",
  "H_Day515",
  "H_Day1530",
  "C_Day02",
  "C_Day25",
  "C_Day515",
  "C_Day1530",
  "Sig_Sp_inter_Traj02",
  "Sig_Sp_inter_Traj25",
  "Sig_Sp_inter_Traj515",
  "Sig_Sp_inter_Traj1530"
)


testing_alluvial <- TopTable %>%
  filter(
    Symbol %in% gene_list,
    Comparisons %in% comparison_order
  ) %>%
  mutate(
    Direction = case_when(
      adj.P.Val < 0.05 & logFC > 0 ~ "Up",
      adj.P.Val < 0.05 & logFC < 0 ~ "Down",
      TRUE ~ "Not significant"
    ),
    Comparison_Group = case_when(
      grepl("^HvC", Comparisons) ~ "Species",
      grepl("^H_Day", Comparisons) ~ "Human",
      grepl("^C_Day", Comparisons) ~ "Chimp",
      grepl("^Sig_Sp_inter", Comparisons) ~ "Interaction",
      TRUE ~ "Other"
    ),
    Comparisons = factor(
      Comparisons,
      levels = comparison_order
    )
  )

plot_testing_alluvial <- function(data, group_to_plot) {
  
  plot_data <- data %>%
    filter(Comparison_Group == group_to_plot)
  
  ggplot(
    plot_data,
    aes(
      x = Comparisons,
      stratum = Direction,
      alluvium = Symbol,
      y = 1,
      fill = Direction
    )
  ) +
    geom_alluvium(alpha = 0.5) +
    geom_stratum() +
    theme_bw() +
    theme(
      axis.text.x = element_text(angle = 45, hjust = 1)
    ) +
    labs(
      x = NULL,
      y = paste0("Number of ",testing),
      title = group_to_plot
    )
}



Species_plot <- plot_testing_alluvial(
  testing_alluvial,
  "Species"
)

Human_plot <- plot_testing_alluvial(
  testing_alluvial,
  "Human"
)

Chimp_plot <- plot_testing_alluvial(
  testing_alluvial,
  "Chimp"
)

Interaction_plot <- plot_testing_alluvial(
  testing_alluvial,
  "Interaction"
)

  filter(
    Symbol %in% gene_list,
    Comparisons %in% comparison_order
  ) %>%
  mutate(
    Direction = case_when(
      adj.P.Val < 0.05 & logFC > 0 ~ "Up",
      adj.P.Val < 0.05 & logFC < 0 ~ "Down",
      TRUE ~ "Not significant"
    ),
    Comparison_Group = case_when(
      grepl("^HvC", Comparisons) ~ "Species",
      grepl("^H_Day", Comparisons) ~ "Human",
      grepl("^C_Day", Comparisons) ~ "Chimp",
      grepl("^Sig_Sp_inter", Comparisons) ~ "Interaction",
      TRUE ~ "Other"
    ),
    Comparisons = factor(
      Comparisons,
      levels = comparison_order
    )
  )

plot_testing_alluvial <- function(data, group_to_plot) {
  
  plot_data <- data %>%
    filter(Comparison_Group == group_to_plot)
  
  ggplot(
    plot_data,
    aes(
      x = Comparisons,
      stratum = Direction,
      alluvium = Symbol,
      y = 1,
      fill = Direction
    )
  ) +
    geom_alluvium(alpha = 0.5) +
    geom_stratum() +
    theme_bw() +
    theme(
      axis.text.x = element_text(angle = 45, hjust = 1)
    ) +
    labs(
      x = NULL,
      y = paste0("Number of ",testing),
      title = group_to_plot
    )
}

TopTable <- readRDS("data/DGE/TopTable_Symbols_Filt_logcpm_G1_SpTp_RNA_fc_NoRep_No4.RDS")
TopTable_HvC_DEG_Fate <- readRDS("data/DGE/TopTable_HvC_DEG_Fate.RDS")
KZFPs_list <- readRDS("data/TEs/KZFPs_list.RDS")

# Add fate annotation to full TopTable
TopTable_Fate <- TopTable %>%
  filter(
    Comparisons %in% c(
      "HvC_Day0",

# FDR correction within each HvC comparison
Fate_KZFP_results <- Fate_KZFP_results %>%
  group_by(Comparison) %>%
  mutate(
    FDR = p.adjust(Fisher_p, method = "BH")
  ) %>%
  ungroup()


# Order developmental stages

  "HvC_Day0",
  "HvC_Day2",
  "HvC_Day5",
  "HvC_Day15",
  "HvC_Day30"
)


Fate_KZFP_results <- Fate_KZFP_results %>%
  mutate(
    Comparison = factor(
      Comparison,
      levels = comparison_order
    ),
    Significant = FDR < 0.05
  )

TopTable <- readRDS("data/DGE/TopTable_Symbols_Filt_logcpm_G1_SpTp_RNA_fc_NoRep_No4.RDS")
TopTable_HvC_DEG_Fate <- readRDS("data/DGE/TopTable_HvC_DEG_Fate.RDS")

ZNFs <- readRDS("data/TEs/ZNFs.RDS")

ZNFs_list <- ZNFs$`Approved symbol`

KZFPs <- readRDS("data/TEs/KZFPs.RDS")

KZFPs_list <- KZFPs$Label

overlap <- intersect(ZNFs_list, KZFPs_list)

gene_set <- setdiff(ZNFs_list, overlap)

# Add fate annotation to full TopTable
TopTable_Fate <- TopTable %>%
  filter(
    Comparisons %in% c(
      "H_Day02", "H_Day25","H_Day515", "H_Day1530",
      "C_Day02", "C_Day25","C_Day515", "C_Day1530"
    )
  ) %>%
  left_join(
    TopTable_HvC_DEG_Fate %>%
      dplyr::select(Gene, Comparisons, Fate),
    by = c("Gene", "Comparisons")
  ) %>%
  mutate(
    Fate = ifelse(is.na(Fate), "Non-DEG", Fate)
  )

comparisons <- unique(TopTable_Fate$Comparisons)

Fate_ZNF_results <- test_fate_enrichment(
  comparisons = unique(TopTable_Fate$Comparisons),
  TopTable_Fate = TopTable_Fate,
  gene_list = gene_set
)


# FDR correction within each HvC comparison
Fate_ZNF_results <- Fate_ZNF_results %>%
  group_by(Comparison) %>%
  mutate(
    FDR = p.adjust(Fisher_p, method = "BH")
  ) %>%
  ungroup()


# Order developmental stages
comparison_order <- c(
  "H_Day02", "H_Day25","H_Day515", "H_Day1530",
      "C_Day02", "C_Day25","C_Day515", "C_Day1530"
)


Fate_ZNF_results <- Fate_ZNF_results %>%
  mutate(
    Comparison = factor(
      Comparison,
      levels = comparison_order
    ),
    Significant = FDR < 0.05
  )

KZFP
TopTable_HvC_DEG_Fate <- readRDS("data/DGE/TopTable_HvC_DEG_Fate.RDS")
RNA_sets <- readRDS("data/DGE/RNA_sets_Filt_logcpm_G1_SpTp_RNA_fc_NoRep_No4.RDS")

KZFPs_list <- readRDS("data/TEs/KZFPs_list.RDS")
TopTable <- readRDS(
  "data/DGE/TopTable_Symbols_Filt_logcpm_G1_SpTp_RNA_fc_NoRep_No4.RDS"
)

library(dplyr)
library(stringr)

timepoints <- c("Day0","Day2","Day5","Day15","Day30")

df <- TopTable %>%
  filter(Symbol %in% KZFPs_list) %>% 
  filter(str_detect(Comparisons, "^HvC_Day")) %>%
  mutate(
    Time = str_extract(Comparisons, "Day[0-9]+"),
    DEG = adj.P.Val < 0.05
  ) %>%
  filter(DEG)

genes_list <- list(
  Day0  = df %>% filter(Time == "Day0") %>% pull(Gene) %>% unique(),
  Day2  = df %>% filter(Time == "Day2") %>% pull(Gene) %>% unique(),
  Day5  = df %>% filter(Time == "Day5") %>% pull(Gene) %>% unique(),
  Day15 = df %>% filter(Time == "Day15") %>% pull(Gene) %>% unique(),
  Day30 = df %>% filter(Time == "Day30") %>% pull(Gene) %>% unique()
)

ggVennDiagram(genes_list, label = "count") +
  scale_fill_gradient(low = "white", high = "darkgreen") +
  theme(
    panel.grid = element_blank(),
    panel.background = element_blank(),
    axis.text = element_text(size = 10),
    plot.title = element_text(face = "bold")
  )

Version Author Date
ec7545b John D. Hurley 2026-08-24
565c3ec John D. Hurley 2026-08-23
fcf4060 John D. Hurley 2026-08-21 
df <- TopTable %>%
  filter(Symbol %in% KZFPs_list) %>% 
  filter(str_detect(Comparisons, "^HvC_Day")) %>%
  mutate(
    Stage = str_extract(Comparisons, "Day[0-9]+"),
    DEG = adj.P.Val < 0.05
  ) %>%
  filter(DEG)

genes <- list(
  Day0  = df %>% filter(Stage == "Day0") %>% pull(Gene) %>% unique(),
  Day2  = df %>% filter(Stage == "Day2") %>% pull(Gene) %>% unique(),
  Day5  = df %>% filter(Stage == "Day5") %>% pull(Gene) %>% unique(),
  Day15 = df %>% filter(Stage == "Day15") %>% pull(Gene) %>% unique(),
  Day30 = df %>% filter(Stage == "Day30") %>% pull(Gene) %>% unique()
)

days <- names(genes)

shared_all <- Reduce(intersect, genes)

get_fate <- function(day) {

  g <- genes[[day]]

  specific <- setdiff(
    g,
    unlist(genes[names(genes) != day])
  )

  shared_all_day <- intersect(
    g,
    shared_all
  )

  shared_partial <- setdiff(
    setdiff(g, specific),
    shared_all_day
  )

  data.frame(
    Day = day,
    Specific = length(specific),
    Shared_partial = length(shared_partial),
    Shared_all = length(shared_all_day)
  )
}

plot_df <- do.call(
  rbind,
  lapply(names(genes), get_fate)
)

library(tidyr)

plot_long <- plot_df %>%
  pivot_longer(
    cols = c(Specific, Shared_partial, Shared_all),
    names_to = "Category",
    values_to = "Count"
  )

plot_long$Day <- factor(
  plot_long$Day,
  levels = c("Day0","Day2","Day5","Day15","Day30")
)

library(ggplot2)

ggplot(plot_long, aes(x = Day, y = Count, fill = Category)) +
  geom_col() +
  scale_fill_manual(values = c(
    "Specific" = "#D55E00",
    "Shared_partial" = "#0072B2",
    "Shared_all" = "#009E73"
  )) +
  theme_bw() +
  labs(
    x = "Developmental stage",
    y = "Number of KZFP DEGs",
    fill = "",
    title = "Fate of HvC KZFP DEGs"
  )

565c3ec John D. Hurley 2026-08-23
fcf4060 John D. Hurley 2026-08-21 
library(dplyr)
library(tidyr)

plot_long_prop <- plot_df %>%
  pivot_longer(
    cols = c(Specific, Shared_partial, Shared_all),
    names_to = "Category",
    values_to = "Count"
  ) %>%
  group_by(Day) %>%
  mutate(
    Prop = Count / sum(Count)
  ) %>%
  ungroup()

plot_long_prop$Day <- factor(
  plot_long_prop$Day,
  levels = c("Day0","Day2","Day5","Day15","Day30")
)

library(ggplot2)

ggplot(plot_long_prop, aes(x = Day, y = Prop, fill = Category)) +
  geom_col() +
  scale_fill_manual(values = c(
    "Specific" = "#D55E00",
    "Shared_partial" = "#0072B2",
    "Shared_all" = "#009E73"
  )) +
  theme_bw() +
  labs(
    x = "Developmental stage",
    y = "Proportion of KZFP DEGs",
    fill = "",
    title = "Fate of HvC KZFP DEGs (normalized)"
  )

  filter(str_detect(Comparisons, "^HvC_Day")) %>%
  mutate(
    Stage = str_extract(Comparisons, "Day[0-9]+"),
    DEG = adj.P.Val < 0.05
  ) %>%
  filter(DEG)

genes <- list(
  Day0  = df %>% filter(Stage == "Day0") %>% pull(Gene) %>% unique(),
  Day2  = df %>% filter(Stage == "Day2") %>% pull(Gene) %>% unique(),
  Day5  = df %>% filter(Stage == "Day5") %>% pull(Gene) %>% unique(),
  Day15 = df %>% filter(Stage == "Day15") %>% pull(Gene) %>% unique(),
  Day30 = df %>% filter(Stage == "Day30") %>% pull(Gene) %>% unique()
)

days <- names(genes)

shared_all <- Reduce(intersect, genes)

get_fate <- function(day) {

  g <- genes[[day]]

  specific <- setdiff(
    g,
    unlist(genes[names(genes) != day])
  )

  shared_all_day <- intersect(
    g,
    shared_all
  )

  shared_partial <- setdiff(
    setdiff(g, specific),
    shared_all_day
  )

  data.frame(
    Day = day,
    Specific = length(specific),
    Shared_partial = length(shared_partial),
    Shared_all = length(shared_all_day)
  )
}

plot_df <- do.call(
  rbind,
  lapply(names(genes), get_fate)
)

library(tidyr)

plot_long <- plot_df %>%
  pivot_longer(
    cols = c(Specific, Shared_partial, Shared_all),
    names_to = "Category",
    values_to = "Count"
  )

plot_long$Day <- factor(
  plot_long$Day,
  levels = c("Day0","Day2","Day5","Day15","Day30")
)

library(ggplot2)

ggplot(plot_long, aes(x = Day, y = Count, fill = Category)) +
  geom_col() +
  scale_fill_manual(values = c(
    "Specific" = "#D55E00",
    "Shared_partial" = "#0072B2",
    "Shared_all" = "#009E73"
  )) +
  theme_bw() +
  labs(
    x = "Developmental stage",
    y = "Number of TFs-ZNF&KZFP DEGs",
    fill = "",
    title = "Fate of HvC TFs-ZNF&KZFP DEGs across development"
  )

library(dplyr)
library(tidyr)

plot_long_prop <- plot_df %>%
  pivot_longer(
    cols = c(Specific, Shared_partial, Shared_all),
    names_to = "Category",
    values_to = "Count"
  ) %>%
  group_by(Day) %>%
  mutate(
    Prop = Count / sum(Count)
  ) %>%
  ungroup()

plot_long_prop$Day <- factor(
  plot_long_prop$Day,
  levels = c("Day0","Day2","Day5","Day15","Day30")
)

library(ggplot2)

ggplot(plot_long_prop, aes(x = Day, y = Prop, fill = Category)) +
  geom_col() +
  scale_fill_manual(values = c(
    "Specific" = "#D55E00",
    "Shared_partial" = "#0072B2",
    "Shared_all" = "#009E73"
  )) +
  theme_bw() +
  labs(
    x = "Developmental stage",
    y = "Proportion of TFs-ZNF&KZFP DEGs",
    fill = "",
    title = "Fate of HvC TFs-ZNF&KZFP DEGs across development (normalized)"
  )

### Trajectory

KZFP_Toptable <- readRDS("data/TEs/KZFPs_Toptable.RDS")

KZFP_Toptable_sig <- KZFP_Toptable %>% 
  filter(adj.P.Val < 0.05)

# write.csv(KZFP_Toptable_sig, "KZFP_Toptable_sig.csv", row.names = FALSE)

comparisons <- unique(KZFP_Toptable_sig$Comparisons)

for (comparison in comparisons) {
  
  KZFP_Toptable_Higher <- KZFP_Toptable %>% 
    filter(Comparisons == comparison) %>% 
    filter(adj.P.Val < 0.05) %>% 
    filter(logFC > 0)
  
  KZFP_Toptable_Lower <- KZFP_Toptable %>% 
    filter(Comparisons == comparison) %>% 
    filter(adj.P.Val < 0.05) %>% 
    filter(logFC < 0)
  
  assign(
    paste0("KZFP_Toptable_", comparison, "_Higher"),
    KZFP_Toptable_Higher
  )
  
  assign(
    paste0("KZFP_Toptable_", comparison, "_Lower"),
    KZFP_Toptable_Lower
  )
}

Reduce(
  intersect,
  list(
    KZFP_Toptable_HvC_Day0_Higher$Symbol,
    KZFP_Toptable_HvC_Day2_Higher$Symbol,
    KZFP_Toptable_HvC_Day5_Higher$Symbol,
    KZFP_Toptable_HvC_Day15_Higher$Symbol,
    KZFP_Toptable_HvC_Day30_Higher$Symbol
  )
)

 [8] "ZNF71"   "ZNF554"  "ZNF439"  "ZNF749"  "ZNF214"  "ZKSCAN3"
Reduce(
  intersect,
  list(
    KZFP_Toptable_HvC_Day0_Lower$Symbol,
    KZFP_Toptable_HvC_Day2_Lower$Symbol,
    KZFP_Toptable_HvC_Day5_Lower$Symbol,
    KZFP_Toptable_HvC_Day15_Lower$Symbol,

  )

Proportions

KZFPs
TopTable <- readRDS("data/DGE/TopTable_Symbols_Filt_logcpm_G1_SpTp_RNA_fc_NoRep_No4.RDS")
RNA_sets <- readRDS("data/DGE/RNA_sets_Filt_logcpm_G1_SpTp_RNA_fc_NoRep_No4.RDS")
KZFP_list <- readRDS("data/TEs/KZFPs_list.RDS")


traj_levels <- c("H_Day02","H_Day25","H_Day515","H_Day1530",
                 "C_Day02","C_Day25","C_Day515","C_Day1530")

df <- TopTable %>%
  dplyr::filter(Comparisons %in% traj_levels) %>% 
  dplyr::filter(Symbol %in% KZFP_list)

df <- df %>%
  dplyr::mutate(
    Species = ifelse(grepl("^H", Comparisons), "Human", "Chimp"),
    Stage = sub(".*_(Day[0-9]+)", "\\1", Comparisons),
    DEG = adj.P.Val < 0.05
  )

df_deg <- df %>%
  dplyr::filter(DEG)

library(dplyr)

df_deg2 <- df_deg %>%
  group_by(Gene, Stage, Species) %>%
  summarise(.groups = "drop")

df_overlap <- df_deg2 %>%
  tidyr::pivot_wider(
    names_from = Species,
    values_from = Gene,
    values_fn = length,
    values_fill = 0
  )

df_flags <- df_deg2 %>%
  mutate(flag = TRUE) %>%
  tidyr::pivot_wider(
    names_from = Species,
    values_from = flag,
    values_fill = FALSE
  ) %>%
  dplyr::mutate(
    category = case_when(
      Human & Chimp ~ "Shared",
      Human ~ "Human only",
      Chimp ~ "Chimp only"
    )
  )

plot_df <- df_flags %>%
  group_by(Stage, category) %>%
  summarise(n = n(), .groups = "drop") %>%
  group_by(Stage) %>%
  mutate(prop = n / sum(n))

plot_df$Stage <- factor(
  plot_df$Stage,
  levels = c("Day02", "Day25", "Day515", "Day1530")
)

library(ggpattern)

ggplot(plot_df, aes(x = Stage, y = prop, fill = category)) +

  geom_col_pattern(
    aes(pattern = category),
    colour = "black",
    pattern_colour = "black",
    pattern_fill = "white",
    pattern_density = 0.4,
    pattern_spacing = 0.02
  ) +

  scale_fill_manual(values = c(
    "Shared" = "grey70",
    "Human only" = "black",
    "Chimp only" = "white"
  )) +

  scale_pattern_manual(values = c(
    "Shared" = "stripe",
    "Human only" = "none",
    "Chimp only" = "none"
  )) +

  theme_minimal(base_size = 14) +
  labs(
    x = "Developmental Stage",
    y = "Proportion of KZFP DEGs",
    fill = "",
    pattern = ""
  )

  group_by(Stage, category) %>%
  summarise(n = n(), .groups = "drop") %>%
  group_by(Stage) %>%
  mutate(prop = n / sum(n))

plot_df$Stage <- factor(
  plot_df$Stage,
  levels = c("Day02", "Day25", "Day515", "Day1530")
)

library(ggpattern)

ggplot(plot_df, aes(x = Stage, y = prop, fill = category)) +

  geom_col_pattern(
    aes(pattern = category),
    colour = "black",
    pattern_colour = "black",

    pattern_density = 0.4,

  scale_fill_manual(values = c(

  )

KZFP_DEG_direction <- KZFP_DEG %>%
  mutate(
    Direction = case_when(
      # Species x trajectory interactions
      grepl("^Sig_Sp_inter", Comparisons) & logFC > 0 ~ "Human-Chimp difference increased",
      grepl("^Sig_Sp_inter", Comparisons) & logFC < 0 ~ "Human-Chimp difference decreased",
      
      TRUE ~ "Unknown"
    )
  ) %>%
  dplyr::count(Comparisons, Direction)

TopTable <- readRDS("data/DGE/TopTable_Symbols_Filt_logcpm_G1_SpTp_RNA_fc_NoRep_No4.RDS")
KZFPs_list <- readRDS("data/TEs/KZFPs_list.RDS")
KZFP_metadata_Hardy2023 <- readRDS("data/TEs/KZFPs_ages.RDS")

KZFP_metadata_Hardy2023 <- KZFP_metadata_Hardy2023 %>%
  separate(
    `TE family`,
    into = c("class", "family"),
    sep = "/"
  )

comparisons <- c(
  "Sig_Sp_inter_Traj02",
  "Sig_Sp_inter_Traj25",
  "Sig_Sp_inter_Traj515",
  "Sig_Sp_inter_Traj1530"
)

KZFP_DEG <- TopTable %>%
  filter(
    Comparisons %in% comparisons,
    Symbol %in% KZFPs_list,
    adj.P.Val < 0.05
  ) %>%
  dplyr::select(Gene,logFC, Comparisons,Symbol)


KZFP_Interactions_meta <- KZFP_DEG %>%
  left_join(
    KZFP_metadata_Hardy2023,
    by = c("Symbol" = "KZFP")
  )

comparison_order <- c(
  "Sig_Sp_inter_Traj02",
  "Sig_Sp_inter_Traj25",
  "Sig_Sp_inter_Traj515",
  "Sig_Sp_inter_Traj1530"
)

KZFP_Interactions_meta <- KZFP_Interactions_meta %>%
  mutate(
    Comparisons = factor(
      Comparisons,
      levels = comparison_order
    ))

KZFP_Summary <- KZFP_Interactions_meta %>%
  group_by(Comparisons) %>%
  summarise(
    n_KZFPs = n_distinct(Gene),
    .groups = "drop"
  )

TE_Summary_Class <- KZFP_Interactions_meta %>%
  group_by(Comparisons, class) %>%
  summarise(
    n = n_distinct(Gene),
    .groups = "drop"
  ) %>%
  pivot_wider(
    names_from = class,
    values_from = n,
    values_fill = 0
  ) %>%
  mutate(
    Comparisons = factor(
      Comparisons,
      levels = comparison_order
    )
  ) %>%
  arrange(Comparisons) %>%
  mutate(
    Comparisons = as.character(Comparisons)
  )

dge <- readRDS("data/DGE/dge_logcpm_G1_SpTp_No4_NoRep.RDS")
log2cpm <- cpm(dge, log=TRUE) 


############################
# KZFP EXPRESSION TRAJECTORIES
############################

# Get unique KZFPs identified as interaction DEGs
KZFP_plot_genes <- KZFP_Interactions_meta %>%
  filter(Comparisons == "Sig_Sp_inter_Traj25") %>% 
  filter(Symbol == "ZFP57") %>% 
  distinct(Gene, Symbol)

plot_individual <- log2cpm %>%
  as.data.frame() %>%
  tibble::rownames_to_column(var = "Gene") %>%
  inner_join(KZFP_plot_genes, by = "Gene") %>%
  pivot_longer(
    cols = -c(Gene, Symbol),
    names_to = "sample",
    values_to = "log2cpm"
  ) %>%
  extract(
    sample,
    into = c("Individual", "Day"),
    regex = "^(.*)_Day(\\d+)$"
  ) %>%
  mutate(
    Day = factor(Day, levels = c("0", "2", "5", "15", "30")),
    Species = case_when(
      str_starts(Individual, "H") ~ "Human",
      str_starts(Individual, "C") ~ "Chimpanzee",
      Individual %in% c("17_3", "78_1") ~ "Human",
      TRUE ~ NA_character_
    )
  ) %>%
  group_by(Gene, Symbol, Species, Individual, Day) %>%
  summarise(
    mean_log2cpm = mean(log2cpm, na.rm = TRUE),
    median_log2cpm = median(log2cpm, na.rm = TRUE),
    .groups = "drop"
  )

# Check number of KZFPs
n_distinct(plot_individual$Symbol)
[1] 1
############################
# SPECIES-LEVEL SUMMARIES
############################

plot_data <- plot_individual %>%
  group_by(Gene, Symbol, Species, Day) %>%
  summarise(
    n_individuals = n_distinct(Individual),

    mean_signal = mean(mean_log2cpm, na.rm = TRUE),
    sd_log2cpm = sd(mean_log2cpm, na.rm = TRUE),
    sem_log2cpm = sd_log2cpm / sqrt(n_individuals),

    median_signal = median(median_log2cpm, na.rm = TRUE),
    Q1 = quantile(median_log2cpm, 0.25, na.rm = TRUE),
    Q3 = quantile(median_log2cpm, 0.75, na.rm = TRUE),

    .groups = "drop"
  )


############################
# PLOT 1: MEDIAN + IQR
############################

ggplot(
  plot_data,
  aes(
    x = Day,
    y = median_signal,
    color = Species,
    group = Species
  )
) +
  geom_line(linewidth = 1) +
  geom_point(size = 2.5) +
  geom_errorbar(
    aes(ymin = Q1, ymax = Q3),
    width = 0.15,
    linewidth = 0.6
  ) +
  facet_wrap(~ Symbol, scales = "fixed", ncol = 3) +
  scale_color_manual(
    values = c(
      "Human" = "red",
      "Chimpanzee" = "blue"
    )
  ) +
  scale_x_discrete(drop = FALSE) +
  labs(
    title = "KZFP Expression Trajectories",
    subtitle = "KZFPs identified as species-interaction DEGs",
    x = "Differentiation Day",
    y = "Median Expression (log2 CPM)",
    color = "Species"
  ) +
  theme_bw(base_size = 12) +
  theme(
    plot.title = element_text(face = "bold", hjust = 0.5),
    plot.subtitle = element_text(hjust = 0.5),
    legend.position = "bottom",
    strip.text = element_text(face = "bold")
  )

  geom_point(
    data = plot_individual,

      x = Day,

      color = Species
    ),

    alpha = 0.5,
    size = 1.8
  ) +

    data = plot_individual,
    aes(
      x = Day,
      y = median_log2cpm,

    linewidth = 0.5
  ) +

Alluvial plot

TopTable <- readRDS("data/DGE/TopTable_Symbols_Filt_logcpm_G1_SpTp_RNA_fc_NoRep_No4.RDS")
KZFPs_list <- readRDS("data/TEs/KZFPs_list.RDS")

comparisons <- unique(TopTable$Comparisons)

comparison_order <- c(
  "HvC_Day0",
  "HvC_Day2",
  "HvC_Day5",
  "HvC_Day15",
  "HvC_Day30",
  "H_Day02",
  "H_Day25",
  "H_Day515",
  "H_Day1530",
  "C_Day02",
  "C_Day25",
  "C_Day515",
  "C_Day1530",
  "Sig_Sp_inter_Traj02",
  "Sig_Sp_inter_Traj25",
  "Sig_Sp_inter_Traj515",
  "Sig_Sp_inter_Traj1530"
)


KZFP_alluvial <- TopTable %>%
  filter(
    Symbol %in% KZFPs_list,
    Comparisons %in% comparison_order
  ) %>%
  mutate(
    Direction = case_when(
      adj.P.Val < 0.05 & logFC > 0 ~ "Up",
      adj.P.Val < 0.05 & logFC < 0 ~ "Down",
      TRUE ~ "Not significant"
    ),
    Comparison_Group = case_when(
      grepl("^HvC", Comparisons) ~ "Species",
      grepl("^H_Day", Comparisons) ~ "Human",
      grepl("^C_Day", Comparisons) ~ "Chimp",
      grepl("^Sig_Sp_inter", Comparisons) ~ "Interaction",
      TRUE ~ "Other"
    ),
    Comparisons = factor(
      Comparisons,
      levels = comparison_order
    )
  )

plot_KZFP_alluvial <- function(data, group_to_plot) {
  
  plot_data <- data %>%
    filter(Comparison_Group == group_to_plot)
  
  ggplot(
    plot_data,
    aes(
      x = Comparisons,
      stratum = Direction,
      alluvium = Symbol,
      y = 1,
      fill = Direction
    )
  ) +
    geom_alluvium(alpha = 0.5) +
    geom_stratum() +
    theme_bw() +
    theme(
      axis.text.x = element_text(angle = 45, hjust = 1)
    ) +
    labs(
      x = NULL,
      y = "Number of KZFPs",
      title = group_to_plot
    )
}



Species_plot <- plot_KZFP_alluvial(
  KZFP_alluvial,
  "Species"
)

Human_plot <- plot_KZFP_alluvial(
  KZFP_alluvial,
  "Human"
)

Chimp_plot <- plot_KZFP_alluvial(
  KZFP_alluvial,
  "Chimp"
)

Interaction_plot <- plot_KZFP_alluvial(
  KZFP_alluvial,
  "Interaction"
)
